It didn't seem to start because of anything in particular; this heavy mood just gradually crept up on me about a month ago.\n\nDoctor: How have you been feeling about your daily activities and hobbies over the past month, have you noticed a decrease in interest or pleasure in things you normally enjoy?\n\nPatient: Yes, I've completely lost interest in the things I used to love doing. Over the past month, none of my usual hobbies bring me any joy or pleasure anymore. It all just feels empty.\n\nDoctor: Can you tell me if this is the first time you've experienced a period of persistent low mood and loss of interest, or have you had episodes like this before that lasted for more than two years?\n\nPatient: I'm not really sure if I've experienced something like this in the past. But these current feelings of low mood and loss of interest have been going on for about a month now.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced a period of at least two years with depressive symptoms, either continuously or on and off, to help clarify if this is a new development or part of a longer pattern?\n\nPatient: No, I've never had these feelings last for anything like two years. This is actually quite new to me and has only been going on for about a month.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any changes in your appetite, sleep patterns, energy levels, or experienced any feelings of worthlessness or guilt over the past month?\n\nPatient: Yes, my sleep has been awful—I struggle to fall asleep, wake up constantly through the night, and never feel rested, and my appetite has changed quite a bit too. I also have absolutely no motivation to do anything lately. However, I haven't really been feeling worthless or guilty.\n\nDoctor: Can you tell me more about your past experiences with depression, even if they seemed minor or not as severe as what you're feeling now, to help me understand if this is a new episode or part of a longer-term condition?\n\nPatient: I don't recall ever having past episodes of depression, even minor ones. This is really the first time I have ever felt this way. All of these symptoms just started about a month ago.\n\nDoctor: Have you had any thoughts of death, or suicidal thoughts, over the past month, as this is an important aspect to assess in relation to your current depressive symptoms?\n\nPatient: No, I haven't had any thoughts of death or suicide at all. Thankfully, it hasn't gotten to that point for me.\n\nDoctor: Can you confirm that these feelings of depression have been consistently present for about a month now, without any significant periods of improvement, to help differentiate between a major depressive episode and a persistent depressive disorder?\n\nPatient: Yes, that's correct. These feelings have been there every single day for the past month without any breaks or improvement. I haven't had a single day where I felt like my usual self."
  }
